## Helsinki

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

PLEASE accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

The progress of Kali-yuga is rapidly manifesting. People in general have lost their vision of the noble purpose and goal of human life. Yet even in this part of the world we are receiving daily so many orders for your books, and this naturally gives us a lot of encouragement. It is a great honor for us to be able to embrace your books with a realization that here is the standard knowledge that can penetrate through any kind of life and fault for the next ten thousand years, and that you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are ever present within these teachings, guiding and purifying those who follow them. You have left us with so much, and we know for sure that by taking shelter of your mission of producing and distributing the storehouse of transcendental literature, all success is guaranteed. But as you well know, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because we are so fallen and are sometimes covered over with so many misconceptions, we cannot always appreciate your unlimited mercy. Thus we accept other forms of shelter. So kindly give us the confidence and conviction needed to know that, save and except for your lotus feet, there is no other shelter. To you we owe so much; we will never be able to repay this debt, even if we engage in serving your lotus feet for eternity. You have given us the highest goal, and there is nothing better within the entire creation. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for saving us from this prison of material existence.

*Your worthless servants at ISKCON Finland,*

## Holland

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

PLEASE accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

We are all your granddisciples, and we know you through your representative Bhagavān Goswami Mahārāja, your books and lectures, and your ISKCON movement.

Since we've met you, life is only progressive, we having come from the deepest darkness of ignorance:

*kirāta-hūṇāndra-pulinda-pulkaśā*  
*ābhīra-śumbhā yavanāḥ khasādayalḥ*  
*ye ’nye ca pāpā yad-apāśrayāśrayāḥ*  
*śudhyanti tasmai prabhaviṣṇave namaḥ*

**"Kirāta, Hūṇa, Andhra, Pulina, Pulkaśa, Abhīra, Śumbha, Yavana, members of the Khasa races and even others addicted to sinful acts can be purified by taking shelter of the devotees of the Lord, due to H is being the supreme power. I beg to offer my respectful obeisances unto Him."**

Thanks to you, our lives have become perfect, and they can become more perfect and most perfect by our continuing to follow in your divine footsteps. By reading *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* step by step, cautiously, one is sure to become a God-realized soul, and he will be able to convert others to accept Kṛṣṇa as the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

Your International Society for Krishna Consciousness has the best philosophy, the best political system and plan for organizing society, the best leaders, the best scientists, the best magazine (your *Back to Godhead*), the best and most scientific process for knowing and realizing the Absolute Truth, the best art, the best music, and the best *mantra*: Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. *Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

It is wonderful to be in a movement where everything is wonderful. In Holland, all except a few asuras are anxiously waiting to see the sun of ISKCON rise and dissipate the fog and the rain clouds of atheism.

(Written by Gaurāṅga-prema dāsa)

*We beg to remain your eternal servants,*

## Hong Kong

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

ON THIS MOST auspicious day of your divine appearance within this world, please allow us to offer our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet again and again.

As we meditate on Your Divine Grace, we remember very clearly the words you used in your dedication of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* to describe the relationship you felt with your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Mahārāja: "He lives forever by his divine instructions and the follower lives with him."

We are also experiencing this deep realization of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. For those who follow your instructions without deviation, you are personally present to guide all of their actions. It is you alone who are empowering and enlivening your followers to successfully spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world.

We are witnessing this great miracle day after day. Even though nearly nine years have passed since your physical disappearance, you are still, through your ISKCON movement, providing the only shelter for millions of conditioned souls who are helplessly drowning in the vast ocean of this Kali-yuga. It was foretold that you would be capable of constructing a house in which the whole world could live, and we are seeing practically how ISKCON is that house. All around the world, people of all nationalities and religious backgrounds are taking shelter of your ISKCON movement. You have constructed this house so solidly that it can never be damaged by any outside influence. You have also said that if your descendants follow your instructions strictly and work endlessly to fulfill your desires, ISKCON will gain increasing internal strength as well. Therefore real worship of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is for us to cooperate together to spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement through book distribution, *prasādam* distribution, and *hari-nāma.* If these programs are executed the way you have taught us, success and unity are guaranteed.

We are begging you on this Vyāsa-pūjā day to eternally give us the clear recollection that we are but insignificant servants of Your Divine Grace. Please bless us with the strength to take shelter only of *guru* and Kṛṣṇa, without seeking the shelter of anything independent of your desires. And please empower us to distribute the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to the people of Khasadesh.

*Your servants of the China mission,*

## Houston

Prabhupāda, you walked nightly from Vṛndāvana to Mathurā  
To distribute literature at the tea stands,  
And the moon waited up for you dutifully.  
You composed poetry in Vṛndāvana, laughing that no one would read it because "all that remained of your family was a list of  
names"; and the monkeys outside your window could not help but grin.  
You wrote letters pleading for help and sent them all over India,  
And when the only response you received was negative, you preached to the walls, which offered no arguments or excuses,  
tirelessly standing through every lecture.  
You translated hour upon hour, day after day, alone in your room,  
As the sun looked in over your shoulder, glowing with enlightenment like a bright reader.  
You sang the sweetest bhajanas in the universe, and only the green parakeets were there to accompany you.  
You danced along the Yamunā bank in ecstatic trance for only  
The trees to see, and they swayed in rhythm with upraised limbs.  
You honored simple prasādam "and in so doing glorified Their Lordships  
Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara, "and the crows competed for your remnants.  
You took your morning walks accompanied by a breeze as brisk  
As your gait, fanning your every step of the way.  
You were pained with compassion for the fallen souls, and the whole sky thundered and wept.  
You prayed for the chance to go preach in the West, and the stars pointed the way.  
Prabhupāda, you walked off the Jaladuta and embraced austerity,  
Your greatest wealth. Who was there to greet you?  
As other so-called yogis were content to embrace their wives,  
At a glance you renounced the very opulence  
That they torture themselves life after life just to achieve.  
You left the bowers of Vṛndāvana for the Bowery of New York  
And were grateful for the opportunity.  
You breathed life into flapping dead bodies and then thanked  
Them for their help. You gave away your food to strangers and were overjoyed when they ate it all.  
You stood in line behind hippies, waiting to take your shower,  
And you praised them for becoming brahminical.  
You initiated new bhaktas and took all their sins  
In exchange for their taking a feast you prepared yourself  
For the occasion; and you were happy to wash all the pots after everyone left.  
You gave classes that opened our eyes with the torch of knowledge, and concluded by saying, "Thank you very much."  
You sat with us and chanted your rounds, asking us to "sit properly" in exchange for love of God, and even when we didn't sit  
properly, you delivered us anyway.  
You worked without sleep to solve our problems for us,  
So that we could get the sleep we required.  
You spent your retirement years out of retirement,  
Building Māyāpur and Vṛndāvana for others to retire in.  
You relentlessly defied Time to produce transcendental books for our purification, insisting in your purports  
That you needed to write them for your own purification.  
You were so ill that you couldn't lift your head off the pillow,  
But you derived strength from preaching to us.  
The doctors said you should have been in excruciating pain,  
But you requested kīrtana as your only medicine and were blissful, going to be with Kṛṣṇa.

Prabhupāda, our only consolation is that we are eternally your servants, because any other length of time would be unbearably brief.  
We are dying to live for you, Prabhupāda.  
Please accept us, Prabhupāda, forever glorifying your lotus feet,  
And let us work for you until we work ourselves to death.  
And then let us get up and work for you again, birth after birth.

## Indonesia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

PLEASE accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. Certainly there is no one more worthy of glorification than you, especially on this most auspicious day of your appearance in this world. You have appeared to carry out the mission of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Lord Kṛṣṇa for the same reasons for which They appear from the spiritual world, as stated in the *Bhagavad-gītā* (4.8):

*paritrāṇāya sādhūnāṁ vināśāya ca duṣkṛtām*  
*dharma-saṁsthāpanārthāya sambhavāmi yuge yuge*

You appeared on behalf of Lord Kṛṣṇa to deliver those who were willing to hear your message with submission and thus take to Kṛṣṇa consciousness; to annihilate the impersonal and atheistic propaganda spread by bogus spiritualists and so-called scientists, who falsely preach that life comes from matter; and to establish religious principles, which were all but lost, by compiling the essence of all the revealed scriptures in your Bhaktivedanta purports as the standard for the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement for at least the next ten thousand years.

Without the rain of your causeless mercy, we were all burning in the blazing fire of material existence, performing countless abominable sinful activities, due to ignorance, which would have cast us down from this human form of life into the cycle of birth and death for many *kalpas.*

Without taking the risk to travel all over the world and purely distribute the *mahā-mantra,* we would never have known the blissful chanting and dancing of the *śrī-kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtana,* which is the only panacea for all the faults of the age of Kali. You have given the world the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* and therefore the whole world is indebted to you. Without the light of your books, there is no light in this age of Kali.

Without you, there would never have been so many hundreds of beautiful temples of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā, as embassies of the spiritual world where everyone can take shelter and be trained in the process of devotional service to the Deity of the Lord in His most personal and merciful forms. The whole world was drowning in the hopeless nothingness of *nirviśeṣa-vāda* and *sūnyavāda,* impersonalism and voidism. By the force of your pure devotion, you brought Lord Kṛṣṇa and His associates to appear directly in Their Deity incarnations, proving without a doubt that the Lord is a person, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and *anyone* can see Him and serve Him daily in the temples of ISKCON. You trained local devotees all over the world to serve the Deities, and this is certainly a great wonder which no one could have imagined.

Without you, we would never have tasted the nectarean *prasādam* of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, which purifies the soul of all sinful reactions. Before, in our ignorance, we were daily gobbling down sinful reactions which would have kept us in hell for many *yugas.* Now it is only by your mercy that we have been trained in the blissful art of cooking, offering, and distributing *kṛṣṇa-prasādam,* the food for the soul.

Without you, the loving pastimes of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa would have remained an unknown mystery to people all over the world. You have so expertly presented Kṛṣṇa's pastimes in the *Kṛṣṇa* book, so that anyone can understand the truth without falling prey to the *sahajiyā* philosophy, which is contradictory to the principles of pure devotional service.

Without you, who would have revealed the holy *dhāmas* of Vṛndāvana and Māyāpur to people all over the world, and who would have assisted the Lord in His transcendental pastimes of spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement all over the world, reinstating countless fallen conditioned souls in the service of the Lord, whom they had forgotten for millions of births?

Without you, who else could have acted with such transcendental power as the direct representative of Lord Kṛṣṇa in this world? Therefore all the revealed scriptures glorify you, and all the great saints and *acaryas,* from ancient times down to the present day, have recognized you and are praising your glories.

Without your mercy, we are nothing. We have no devotion. It is only the rays from your moonlike heart reflecting on us which make us seem like devotees. It is only by pleasing you and receiving your blessings that Kṛṣṇa will be pleased with us.

There is no other who can compare with you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your glories are unlimited. Your devotion is unparalleled in the history of the world. Your scholarship is unrivaled. Your purity is untainted. Your pastimes are inconceivable. Your transcendental potency is unlimited. And your mercy is unfathomable.

Although you have disappeared from our physical vision, we know you will live forever in your instructions and your ISKCON, the society of devotees dedicated to following your instructions. Please bless us that we may always have the chance to serve you and your mission in some way, and please bless us that we may never forget your instructions or become offensive to you or to your devotees.

We hope that this humble offering, together with the Indonesian language edition of *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is,* which we are just now publishing, may be pleasing to you on your appearance day.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *ki jaya!*

## Israel

O my beloved master divine,  
Once again to see your visage shine.  
Service in separation is required.  
Still we miss you so, potent to inspire.

Books you write, our favorite delight  
To read, to print, and launch in the fight.  
Victory is ours if we simply repeat.  
Without them we sink in utter defeat.

O Gurudeva, your wisdom perfect,  
Scientific theories—flaws you detect.  
Nuclear demons attempt to end  
Possibility of war—ironic bend.

Our faith in you growing day by day,  
Māyā deludes your sting,  
Your instructions, the sure answer  
To cure world ignorance from its cancer.

Legal, finance, P. R. and the tax—  
Doing the needful is sometimes complex.  
Burden of love—our hearts are appeased,  
Our only solace, knowing you're pleased.

"ISKCON is finished" some men discuss;  
"Organizations are not for us.  
We prefer freedom to do as we please  
Independent from others—just like the breeze.

They can't conceive your preaching vision,  
Igniting worldwide Kṛṣṇa fission.  
It's no secret, you didn 't think small—  
Like Prahlāda and Gaura rescuing all.

ISKCON caravan rolling along  
While jackals howl their jealousy song.  
"Come join us, "is our beckoning breath.  
"Leave this ocean of birth, pain, and death.

When devotees stray, great is the pain.  
Fervently we beg, "Come back again."  
Don't be surprised by who goes away,  
Rather by those who continue to stay.

I'm not very bright, nor very pure,  
Nevertheless, of one thing I'm sure—  
Your desire's the same, you said it before,  
Distributing books shall open each door.

Now a benediction I must ask,  
Shelter at your lotus feet at last.  
Free this wretch from material desire.  
Prabhupāda, please extinguish the fire.

No one but you could accomplish this,  
Again transport me to the land of bliss.  
There Lord Kṛṣṇa I shall once more find  
With you, my beloved master divine.

(Written by Danavir dāsa Vanacārī)

## Japan

**1.** I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is carrying out the order of his spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, by preaching the message of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu in every town and village. His Divine Grace writes, "I have no qualification, but I have taken up the risk just to carry out the order of His Divine Grace. I depend fully on Their \[Rādhā-Govinda's\] mercy, so far away from Vṛndāvana."

**2.** I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who founded the International Society for Krishna Consciousness at 26 Second Avenue, New York, out of his great compassion for the suffering living entities in the material world. Only by his pure desire has ISKCON been established all over the world.

**3.** I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is changing the lives of the conditioned souls by his pure, ecstatic chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra.* "When the *mantra* is chanted by a pure devotee of the Lord in love, it has the greatest efficacy on hearers, and as such this chanting should be heard from the lips of a pure devotee of the Lord, so that immediate effects can be achieved."

**4.** I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who never tolerates the blasphemy of the *māyāvāda* school, which describes the Absolute Truth as impersonal and formless. His Divine Grace is never defeated by their nonsense philosophy and is fully expert at establishing the conclusion of the *bhāgavata* school.

**5.** I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is always engaged in writing his Bhaktivedanta purports in glorification of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, "full of transcendental words directed toward bringing about a revolution in the impious lives of this world's misdirected civilization."

**6.** I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who taught us that life comes not from chance material combinations, as proposed by modern man. His Divine Grace taught us that Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa is the "life of all that lives," and "everything depends on Him, as pearls are strung on a thread."

**7.** I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who performed the greatest miracle by making disciples in the Western world, where illicit sex, meat-eating, intoxication, and gambling are considered normal activities. Not only did His Divine Grace make many disciples, but he also trained them to carry on the mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness after his departure from this world.

**8.** I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who, because of spontaneous love, is always remembering Kṛṣṇa even in the seemingly most difficult situations. His Divine Grace writes, "I do not remember any part of my life when I was forgetful of Kṛṣṇa."

## Johannesburg

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

PLEASE accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to your tireless efforts to bless the fallen conditioned souls of this Kali-yuga, especially ourselves. Service in separation is said to produce the highest experience of transcendental ecstasy, as exemplified by the *gopīs* and Lord Caitanya Himself. In many ways we, on our lowly level, find that separation from you also heightens our realization of your unique qualities, as we are forced to depend on you in ways that previously we didn't have to.

Previously you were always here to resolve disputes or solve problems, and there was certainly a great feeling of dependence among your disciples on your abilities in this regard. Previously you were constantly preaching the message of *bhāgavata-dharma,* defeating atheists, and cementing the philosophical basis of our attachment to the Absolute Truth.

Previously you were personally directing the development of your movement. You made the policies and devised the strategies for the preaching activities, and you set the priority—the printing and distribution of your books as the focal point of our efforts to distribute the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

And, of course, you were present in your personal form, displaying your transcendental beauty and your faultless standard of surrendered service to your Guru Mahārāja, giving us an easily available living focus for our desire to express our worship and neophyte sentiments of love.

Your departure has forced us to come to a higher standard in our relationship with you. Gone is the personal association, the personal standard, the personal direction, which were like spiritual catalysts in our once-dull lives. Now, just in order to survive in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we have to realize that you are fully present in your instructions, in your mission, in remembrance of your pastimes.

What to speak of just surviving, our experience is that in absorbing ourselves in these activities we see your potencies entering the hearts of your devotees, giving them the ability to practice and preach your message in a way they have never been able to before. Every day your influence is carried deeper and deeper into the heart of Kali, as more books go out and more conditioned souls find themselves inextricably drawn towards this movement.

Separation from you certainly initially seemed like a very perilous footstep we had to take on our path of devotional service, but we are realizing now that, on the transcendental platform, it is not really separation, but part of a lesson we have to learn to become fit to gain your association eternally.

## Johannesburg II

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

PLEASE accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet on the ninety-first anniversary of your most auspicious appearance in this world.

Since we have begun to engage in *sādhana-bhakti* under your order, we are feeling less disturbed by the actions and reactions of material nature. Therefore, on behalf of the devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa now residing in our *prabhu-datta-deśa,* I beg to submit a poem for your pleasure.

All attention to Lord Kṛṣṇa  
He's the light of the moon and sun  
You did give our ears the words He had spoken  
Truth will rain on everyone.  
Since we didn't know of Kṛṣṇa  
Our lives were dry—full of mistake  
The relief you gave made an impression so deep  
Now it should grow; your mercy should not wait.

If we try to hear Lord Kṛṣṇa  
Clarified by your purports  
Then all may understand the master plan you made  
They may take, and lend their full support.  
Our eyes now turn to see Lord Kṛṣṇa  
At the break of every day  
Your purifying life has planted seeds of bhakti in our minds  
Where God again makes His debut.  
We owe our life to you, our master,  
To take out your best for us and all  
The fools rotting in forgetfulness  
Into your hands we 'II safely fall.  
And then, we can get back to Kṛṣṇa  
The goal of the souls shall be fulfilled  
We know that God is here and there and everywhere  
In this way we shall be always thrilled.  
We only know Vṛndāvana  
To a very small degree.  
By the mercy of Your Divine Grace  
Śrī Kṛṣṇa's līlās can be seen.  
So we want to always remember you  
Appearing and acting before our eyes  
To lead us on the path of love of God  
Increase the population in the spiritual sky.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!*

*(Written by Haṁsagati dāsa)*

## Knoxville

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

HOW DO I OFFER sincerely heartfelt praise of you when I am ninety-five percent insincere? In spite of my deficiency, let me try to do so on this opportune occasion offered by you for my purification.

In *The Nectar of Instruction* Prabhupāda states that one should not simply see the external body or feature of the spiritual master or pure devotee, but one should look deeper, to see how the person is engaged in the transcendental service of the Lord. In the *Ādi-līlā* purports Prabhupāda explains that the spiritual master is to be accepted as an associate of Rādhārāṇī or as an associate of Nityānanda Prabhu. We hear of Nityānanda Prabhu's extraordinary quality of mercy toward the fallen souls. Even though, in this age, the fallen souls are so hellishly misguided that they have no interest in Kṛṣṇa or Gaurāṅga, He searches them out and begs them to take to the service of Lord Caitanya. His transcendental form is so wonderful and ecstatic that many persons who even just see Him become converted to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We also saw that when Prabhupāda was present among us, we behaved our best and were inspired to amazing degrees by his example and his words.

I know that without my Godbrothers and Prabhupāda I am finished. All glories to Śrī Guru and Gauranga. All glories to His Divine Grace Śrī Śrīmad Aṣṭottara-śata A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

(Written by Caturvyūha dāsa)

## Laguna Beach

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

PLEASE accept our humble obeisances. Thank you for saving us by engaging us in your service. Service to you is the only real life. Our independence from that position is our downfall. Service to you, according to your instruction and under the direction of proper authority, is the difference between material and spiritual, the difference between speculation and sentimental hallucination and genuine spiritual enthusiasm and ecstasy. Authorized service to you is the difference between the numberless mundane pseudoreligious institutions and the actual transcendental movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the only light in the world. Service to you is the difference between useless and useful, between an empty show, a disturbance in society, and favorable devotional service. It is the difference between being with Kṛṣṇa and being alone. As you have said, this service which you have given us is all that we know of love.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for giving us such a nice life of hearing, chanting, and preaching, distributing books and *prasādam*, and serving Śrī Pañca-tattva. This is the most wonderful life. We are your disciples, by your mercy, but we don't at all feel qualified to serve you or be with you. Actually, we can't understand you at all. We don't think of ourselves as your exclusive servants. We are simply aspiring to become the servant of the servant of your servant. If Kṛṣṇa will accept a little service in this way, that will be a great benediction for us. You have kindly placed us under the care of your G.B.C. and temple authorities, and we feel safe and connected to you in this way. How could we want more than this? There is no higher happiness than preaching on your behalf, with our minds absorbed in you. There is no greater goal than to perfectly represent you in every way.

We are praying for the day when we will no longer be attracted by any subtle or gross forms of materialism, when there will be no bewilderment due to pride and false prestige, and when we will have a drop of potency to convince people of your message.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please allow us to fully purify ourselves by serving you, and please give us the benediction that we will never, ever leave your service.

*Your servants of Śrī Gaurāṅga Purī, ISKCON Laguna Beach.*

## Lake Huntington

To His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda on this most auspicious occasion of his appearance day.

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet!

Taking leave of Lord Jagannātha, smiling serenely,  
Nestled with His devotees  
In the rolling verdant hills  
Of the picturesque Catskill Mountains of the northeast

Driving two and a half hours to offer obeisances  
To Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda  
Brought here from far-off Jaipur  
To purify the most important city in the world

Morning at Newark airport; thoughts of Jersey's Goura-Nitāi,  
Then flying across country,  
Forty-minute lay-over  
In Baltimore, the city of Nitāi-Gaurasundara

People, earphones, senses materially engrossed,  
Merrily on their way to hell  
One, with earphones, being saved  
Hearing you speak the Vedic truths for three thousand miles

L.A. and an all-too-brief glimpse of Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhīśa  
Then three and a half hours north  
Into Pañca-tattva's domain;  
Gāyatrī in the Sierra Nevada's icy waters

Sitting at a meeting with bodies of all descriptions  
Coming from around the world  
Here to implement your plan  
To revolutionize a misguided society

Four-fifteen a.m.; the chanting of the Lord's holy name,  
Then hearing your Bhāgavatam  
Apply the Vedic conclusions  
Perfectly here and now; and for the next ten thousand years

Personal ecstasies, the Bhaktivedanta purports  
Describe the village sannyāsī  
Making disciples in one place  
And proudly proclaiming himself to be "jagad-guru"

The offering: an experience to glorify you  
A contrast with that sannyāsī,  
A tiny glimpse of your position,  
The world ācārya, Jagad-guru Śrīla Prabhupāda!

(Written by Muralīvādaka dāsa)

Humbly submitted by your sons, daughters, and grandchildren at New Puruṣottama-kṣetra.

## London

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

In 1969 you came to London and discovered Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa already waiting here for you, and you mercifully installed Them in a temple next to the British Museum. Then you taught your few disciples the rudiments of Deity worship and left Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in their care while you traveled the world. Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa's clothes were changed weekly, and Their altar stood on a carpet in those days, but each summer you returned and further directed us how to improve our standard of worship.

In 1973, when inspired by you George Harrison gave us Bhaktivedanta Manor ("All glories to George Harrison," you solemnly intoned at the *praṇāma* prayers after *ārati),* you installed beautiful Rādhā-Gokulānanda at the Manor, all the way from the Delhi *paṇḍāl,* rather than take Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa from London. At that time you named our London Kṛṣṇa as Londonīśvara, "the Controller of London," so there could be no doubt where Rādhā-Londonīśvara belonged.

In subsequent years, although Bhaktivedanta Manor became your British headquarters, still you always made a point of coming to see Rādhā-Londonīśvara each time you visited. "Now our movement is expanding, and we have so many centers all over the world," you said, "so I am not able to spend much time with you. But I am praying to Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa to give you the intelligence to look after Them nicely."

During your final visit to England in 1977, you stayed at Bhaktivedanta Manor as usual. However, despite your invalid condition, you came to have the *darśana* of Rādhā-Londonīśvara one last time. You spent twenty minutes with us then in front of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa on the afternoon of Janmāṣṭamī, Their tiny temple room packed with inquisitive, wide-eyed devotees. At that time you remained silent, too weak to talk, and shared with us your tears of ecstasy.

In the last nine years there have been improvements and a general expansion throughout the *yātrā.* However, here at the lotus feet of Rādhā-Londonīśvara not a lot has changed. Although the temple is larger, it is still far too small, and although our preaching has expanded, we have still hardly begun. We do have the restaurant on Oxford Street, which you always wanted, and the Oxford Street *hari-nāma* party still goes out regularly, sounding the holy name around the world. Lord Jagannātha is back and coming up to His eighteenth annual Rathayātrā in London. We are distributing your books in increasing quantities and daily give full *prasādam* to between twenty and forty guests at our lunch program.

But it would be foolish for us to think that we have yet come anywhere close to fulfilling your wishes or expectations. What about the temple in Regents Park? What about the ten temples scattered over London? What about the theater that would win the hearts of the British people? What about the "British Empire" of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and the set of books in every home? You placed Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in a temple in London so that They would one day be known the world over as Rādhā-Londonīśvara, the Supreme Controllers of London. Now we are simply praying to you that you may bestow upon us the power and purity to work untiringly and with full dedication to bring about that day.

*Your insignificant servants at the feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara,*

## Long Island

There is no one as merciful as you,  
sacrificing material comforts.  
You appeared in our midst  
to take us to Kṛṣṇa's lotus feet.  
Please allow us to always serve you.

We were living without knowledge,  
so-called teachers who were only cheaters.  
The living dead; we were so upset.  
No-one seemed to know the answers.  
Please allow us to always serve you.

Crying for someone to guide us  
to the supreme destination, back home.  
You came like the sun  
dissipating the darkness with light.  
Please allow us to always serve you.

You touched our souls  
with your instructions  
forever fresh.  
The soul never meets with death.  
Please allow us to always serve you.

We surrendered to you and your ISKCON.  
It was so nice,  
devotees, prasādam, plays, saṅkīrtana;  
Hare Kṛṣṇa, Rādhā-Govinda.  
Years went by, and you were our general.  
Please allow us to always serve you.

Under you we were fearless.  
Books are the basis  
Utility is the principle  
Purity is the force  
Preaching is the essence.  
Please allow us to always serve you.

We thought you would never leave  
but in November 1977 you left us.  
Our hearts were broken.  
We cried incessantly. What should we do now?  
Please allow us to always serve you.

You had left our external vision.  
Now we had only your instruction.  
Young spiritual children  
losing our loving / other and eternal guide.  
Please allow us to always serve you.

What must we do?  
Will it go on  
without your presence?  
Will it go on?  
Please allow us to always serve you.

"You will show your love for me  
by cooperating together."  
These words ring in our hearts.  
Actually, this is only the start.  
Please allow us to always serve you.

A worldwide preaching mission,  
directed by the Governing Body Commission.  
It will certainly be successful  
if the leaders follow your direction.  
Please allow us to always serve you.

Chanting, dancing, feasting, and preaching.  
A wonderful process for one and all.  
United we stand,  
separate we fall.  
Please allow us to always serve you.

Soon our life will come to an end.  
Our real success will actually depend  
on your satisfaction,  
you being Kṛṣṇa's mercy manifestation.  
Please allow us to always serve you.

## Los Angeles

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

PLEASE accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. In your *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* purports you have written: "According to the laws of nature, when one is extremely opulent one becomes degraded, and this is true both individually and collectively.... It is thus advised that the American people regret their mistaken advancement in civilization. They should take advice from the spiritual master, the representative of Kṛṣṇa. If they do so, they will be happy, and there will be an ideal nation to lead the entire world." *(Bhāg.* 6.7.12, purport)

Here in Los Angeles, most of your disciples and granddisciples are Americans. We know from your teachings, however, that we are spirit souls and that these souls are beyond all kinds of temporary, bodily designations. Our permanent identity is that we are all eternal servants of Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Yet at the same time, we find ourselves in the bodies of American men and women. Somehow or other, by the arrangement of the Supersoul, we have been placed in these American bodies as a material reward and punishment for our past pious and impious activities. Yet by your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have been given the chance to use these American bodies in the devotional service of Kṛṣṇa, to deliver ourselves and our countrymen from the cycle of birth and death.

Your Divine Grace has many times spoken of your high hopes for the American nation. As your disciples and granddisciples, we accept your desires as our life and soul. So we should be constantly thinking how we can help you awaken the American people to the pure Kṛṣṇa conscious ideals and practices that will make them worthy to lead the entire world to a new era of peace and happiness and victory over the miseries afflicting humanity. Making America Kṛṣṇa conscious will be the perfection of our Americanism.

Despite its material advancement, which is envied and emulated the world over, American society remains plagued with conflict, strife, and dissatisfaction. In September of 1965, you sailed into this glittering wasteland devoid of knowledge of Kṛṣṇa, apparently alone and friendless, to start the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. You did not, like so many others, come to this country with an advance publicity campaign and the intention to defraud and mislead the American people with bogus so-called *yoga* and meditation systems that allow one to continue all sorts of sinful activities and award the delusion that one has become one with God. Instead you came to humbly yet boldly preach the Absolute Truth as it is, and you requested that we come up to the actual standard of personal behavior and character necessary to realize our original spiritual nature.

Americans are anxious to create a peaceful world situation through political, military, and diplomatic maneuvering, but you ask how people engaged every day in the slaughter of innocent animals and other living creatures can possibly expect peace. Therefore you demand that we give up eating the flesh of slaughtered animals. Americans want to feel themselves part of an enlightened society of well-bred, intelligent people. But how can unwanted progeny, born of illicit sex, be considered well bred? And how can men and women whose standard of enjoyment is no higher than that of dogs and hogs be considered intelligent or enlightened? You therefore ask that we give up illicit sex life. And furthermore, you question the need for supposedly intelligent and enlightened people to poison their consciousness with drugs and intoxicants of all description. On your order we have given up our base experiments with mind-bending pharmaceuticals and herbs and have also given up our useless habits of smoking and drinking. Americans want to achieve success:

Then work honestly, you advise us. Don't gamble. Don't speculate. Don't use your intelligence to cheat and live at the expense of others.

Americans are famous as champions of causes. You have offered us the chance to serve the highest cause, the cause of all causes. You urge us to perform the highest, most beneficial welfare work—the spreading of love of Kṛṣṇa to every town and village in the world.

At present, however, the energies of Americans are misdirected. You have explained, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that the hand cannot be satisfied unless it gives the food it holds to the stomach. Then the hand automatically becomes satisfied. In the same way, as Americans we can never be satisfied until we learn to direct our inherent propensity to serve its proper, natural object—the Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa, who is the source of everything, both animate and inanimate. If you want to water a tree, you pour water on the root, and automatically it is distributed to all the leaves and branches. It does no good to water each branch separately. If we serve Kṛṣṇa, you declare, we can be assured that we are properly serving our family, community, nation, and the world. It is only Kṛṣṇa who has the power to make anyone completely happy.

Many thousands have heeded our call. But when you first came to America, you felt some apprehension. In the Atlanta temple, on the occasion of the appearance day of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, in 1975, you recalled that when you arrived on America's shores you were thinking that when you approached the American people and asked them to please give up their illicit sex, to please give up their eating of meat, to please give up their intoxication, to please give up their gambling, they would reply with a request for you to please go back home to India.

Fortunately for America, you did not go back, and now you have enlisted us to help you in your mission—the deliverance of the Western countries from atheism and voidism. Out of love for you, we have reformed our lives, and in our humble way we are now trying to induce our fellow Americans to do likewise. In this country we have seen the "bankruptcy" of the policy of separating God from government. The result has been the total degradation of society, from top to bottom. Yes, let there be life, let there be liberty, let there be the pursuit of happiness—but not the life of a dog, the liberty of a dog, and the pursuit of a dog's happiness.

Kṛṣṇa will be very pleased if the American people seriously take up this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. If we do, then Kṛṣṇa will certainly be with us, and in *Bhagavad-gītā* it is said that wherever Kṛṣṇa is, there is also opulence, victory, extraordinary power, and morality. If America takes to Kṛṣṇa consciousness, its future is very bright; if not, only darkness and disappointment await the American people.

If you were present here among us today, you would surely be pleased to see how a new generation of your followers, your granddisciples, has taken up the heavy responsibility of maintaining and expanding the programs you initiated during your presence.

(Written by Drutakarmā dāsa)

*Your servants in New Dvārakā,*

## Malaysia

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

PLEASE accept our prostrated humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Your greatness and mercy are yet to be known by the unfortunate blind souls of this age. Gradually, however, your glories will be revealed. So far only the "tip of the iceberg" is visible. By the constant beating of the *bṛhat mṛdaṅga*, being played by those who are adorned by adherence to your instructions, the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is gradually surfacing. We can see here in Malaysia that although when you departed there were no temples or initiated devotees, now by your mercy alone many people are awakening and finding shelter in your ISKCON.

We are all completely dependent upon your mercy, without which we have nothing at all. We therefore beg you to please bestow upon us the intelligence to act in such a way as to always please you.

*Your eternally indebted servants of ISKCON Malaysia,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Māyāpur

*Dearest spiritual master, O glorious Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

ONCE AGAIN the most glorious occasion of the celebration of your divine appearance in this world gives us the opportunity to offer our prayers unto you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have traveled to numerous sacred *tīrthas* and holy places which are populated by scores of *sādhus, yogīs, paṇḍitas,* and gymnasts, but there is something very, very special—totally unique—about the temples and *āśramas* where you reside and are worshiped, be they situated in New York or Vṛndāvana, Moscow or Māyāpur, Hṛṣikeśa or Honolulu. That mood found in these holy places does not exist anywhere else.

By your own pure life, perfect activities, and teachings, you have given us real life—a life of unlimited spiritual happiness, a life to which we can fully dedicate ourselves, knowing that by sacrificing our insignificant, egotistical desires for self-agrandizement and personal sense gratification in order to serve you and expand your mission, we will never be losing anything but will gain unlimitedly. That mood of purity, the feeling of loving reciprocation, and the knowledge that you really love us and appreciate our foolish, insignificant endeavors to please you are to be found nowhere else, in no other temple or *āśrama.* Therefore we can definitely conclude that the holiest, most sacred place of all is that place where we may find the shelter of your lotus feet. And although we are as rotten and lacking in love for you, somewhere deep within the dark, filthy cavernous cavity of our sinful hearts, a hope exists that one day we will feel your presence once again and rejoice in your eternal service. Begging to remain your eternal servants,

*The devotees at your beloved Māyāpur Chandrodaya Mandir.*

## Mexico City

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
It is not possible for any member of this movement  
To do anything within the movement itself  
Without observing your sober and wise image in the background.  
Your pictures are everywhere,  
Echoes of your absolute presence.  
O magnanimous soul!  
Who can resist falling victim  
To your realization of the Vedic knowledge?  
Everyone is attracted to, everyone reads,  
Everyone acquaints himself with your knowledge,  
Everyone ends up washing himself  
In the crystal-clear waters of your books.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Anyone can give evidence  
That just by remembering your appearance and activities  
The overpowering contempt of may a is vanquished,  
As you encourage us and give us confidence  
To follow your instructions.

O majestic Prabhupāda!  
We did not know  
What incomparable blessing  
The world would have  
On your arrival in the West,  
Nevertheless, the prophecy of your advent  
Was already made, some centuries before, by  
Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura: You would found  
And would promote the movement  
That extinguishes the fire  
Of conditioned life.

O eternal Prabhupāda!  
Lord Brahmā is known as "the grandfather,"  
Due to the relation between him  
And the origin of the material creation.  
Similarly, to uncountable generations  
Of Lord Kṛṣṇa's devotees in the future,  
You will stand out in the history of Vaiṣṇavism as  
"The grandfather," due to the relationship  
Between you and the origin of  
The Vaiṣṇava populations in the world.  
Jaya Prabhupāda! Jaya Prabhupāda!

From your very fallen servants of Mexico City,

## Miami Beach

Please allow us to accept your blessings, Srila Prabhupada

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto 'pi*  
*dhyāyan stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri-sandhyaṁ*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**"By the mercy of the spiritual master one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, one cannot make any advancement. Therefore, I should always remember and praise the spiritual master. At least three times a day I should offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of my spiritual master."**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

PLEASE accept our respectful obeisances at your divine lotus feet. Undoubtedly, you are Lord Kṛṣṇa’s most intimate and confidential servitor in this world, and you are always showering your mercy on all of the unfortunate living entities, even though some will accept this mercy and others will not. It is only by your mercy that we have the chance to understand that we should engage in devotional service and give up all desire for personal sense gratification. You have taught us how to avoid the difficult path of going to hell. The unfortunate conditioned souls, due to their not properly understanding the goal of life, are working very hard to satisfy their senses by illegal means (sinful activities). By dint of their entanglement in sinful life, they are suffering greatly, and in the end they will go to hell for more intense suffering. By your causeless mercy on us, we are now properly situated on the path back to Godhead. The very process of purification is "simply joyful," and in the end we will be delivered back home, back to the spiritual world, for even greater happiness in our service to Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us everything because you have given us the means to perfect our human form of life by returning back home, back to Godhead. Still, knowing our forgetful nature, you would always urge us, "Please *accept* my blessings." You have given us everything, but due to ignorance, many times we hesitate to accept your mercy. Therefore we can understand that not only have you shown us your mercy by teaching us the processes of *saṅkīrtana,* Deity worship, hearing *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, and in general cooperating with one another to spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, but you are always showing us this mercy by continuously helping us to *fully accept* what you have given us. If, due to our offenses, you cease to remind and prod us to take full advantage of what mercy you have already given, we will be doomed to failure in our attempt to go back to Godhead.

The living entities always have the free will to choose between Kṛṣṇa and *māyā.* However, *māyā* is very tricky and is expert at robbing our good intelligence. Without your mercy as a bona fide spiritual master, we are certain to fall even more firmly into *māyā’s* grips. Therefore, we repeatedly pray that you will not only allow us but eternally encourage us to accept your merciful blessings, so that we may cooperate fully in your preaching mission and successfully traverse the path back to Godhead.

*Your servants in Miami Beach.*

## Monterrey

*yan-nathyamāno naṭanātha-nāthaḥ*  
*su-maṅgalaṁ paścima-jaṅgaleṣu*  
*gaurāṅgacandraś caraṇaṁ dadhāti*  
*namāmi taṁ śrī-prabhupādadevam*

**"I offer my respectful obeisances to His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. Because of his prayers, Lord Gauracandra, the king of dancers, has placed His auspicious lotus feet in the jungle of the Western countries."**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

PLEASE accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet again and again. Today is the most blessed event. Today in the celestial planets all *kinnaras* and *gandharvas* begin to sing auspicious songs, the *siddhas* and *cāraṇas* offer auspicious prayers, and the *vidyādharīs,* along with the *apsarās,* begin to dance in jubilation, because today is the anniversary of your divine birth.

Five hundred years ago. Lord Caitanyadeva was on this planet. He made one prophecy, that in every town and village the chanting of the holy name would be spread. So you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, descended from Goloka to bring about the fulfillment of this prediction.

You are an eternal associate of Lord Caitanya. *Gaurāṅgera saṅgi-gāne, nitya-siddha kori'mane.* The only purpose of your appearance is to save the conditioned souls of Kali-yuga by giving them *bhakti,* devotional service to the Lord. Therefore you are *śaktyāveśa-avatāra.* This is confirmed in the *śāstra:*

*kali-kālera dharma-kṛṣṇa-nāma saṅkīrtana*  
*kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tara pravartana*

**"The fundamental religious system in the age of Kali is the chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. Unless empowered by Kṛṣṇa, one cannot propagate the** ***saṅkīrtana*** **movement."**

Also, you are worshiped along with Lord Kṛṣṇa: *sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstraiḥ.* You are the genuine *guru,* and you are worshiped in all temples along with Lord Kṛṣṇa, because you are His representative.

So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are praying to you for your grace to enable us to become pure devotees, to never fall down again from devotional service, to never break the four principles, and to forever remain under the wings of your ISKCON, engaging in the distribution of your transcendental books. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, hear our prayers and give us shelter under your divine lotus feet.

*Your servants in Nueva Nṛsiṁha Purī, Monterrey, Mexico,*

**Granddisciples**

## Montreal

The Lord comes to please His devotees and destroy the miscreants.  
He comes in different arms to fulfill His mission.  
He came as Vāmana, first small, then as great as the universe.  
Similarly, Śrīla Prabhupāda came, seemingly as "an insignificant beggar,"  
But he was a transcendental army in himself.  
They taught: "Let the beggar come to America."  
When Śrīla Prabhupāda put his lotus feet on the land,  
The earth became relieved of its burden of sins.  
At least, there was hope to deliver the earth  
From the darkness of Kali.  
With a definite plan to spread the unalloyed process of devotional service,  
You had the vision to see Lord Caitanya's plan.  
Only time was separating the different elements of that plan before they would definitely come together.  
Somehow or other we were called by your mercy to become an active part and parcel of that great plan: 108 temples, millions of  
books, thousand of devotees, pure lectures, all over the world.  
Lord Caitanya's mercy to everyone  
Without discrimination!

Short eight years after your departure,  
The onslaught of Kali is stronger than ever.  
This is a dark age of dissension. . .  
But you gave us your instructions for protection,  
A regulated life versus irregulated life,  
A life full of knowledge versus a life in ignorance,  
An alternative way out from the dark well of material existence.  
You came amongst the hippies to turn them into happies.  
Real peace and love they had no idea of.  
You set everything right, never to be ever the same after.  
You gave us the taste for Kṛṣṇa.  
You gave us the taste for your association.  
You made us attached to you and Kṛṣṇa.  
Clinging to your lotus feet,  
The taste for serving you with all our heart and soul  
Burns our material desires to ashes.  
Your order to preach controls our bodies, minds, and words.  
Now we must walk as you walked,  
We must go where you went,  
We must see the truth that you gave us,  
And give the truth as it is.

The world is going to hell.  
The demons persecute the innocent.  
The devotees must be strong and brave like Bhīṣmadeva, and tolerant like Prahlāda Mahārāja.  
Depending on Kṛṣṇa for protection,  
We beg at your lotus feet for your mercy.

Let us not invent new standards,  
Let us walk in your fearless footsteps.  
Let us become men of ideal character,  
Molding our lives to your Bhaktivedanta purports,  
Teachings, and personal example.  
Let us disregard sinful life  
And fully embrace devotional life.

We have no strength on our own.  
You are our life and soul.  
Let us not imitate you  
But humbly try to serve you.  
Let us see everything in relationship with you.  
Tadīya-upāsanam. Serving you is our success in this life  
And the next.  
We pray to the Lord of our heart  
To let us always remember that all we are made of  
Is your causeless mercy.  
And we don't want to think that one day  
We won't need to depend as fully on your mercy.  
Out of causeless mercy, kindly accept us  
Under the shade of your lotus feet, O Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
And don't ever let us leave you.

(Written by Prābhava-vigraha dāsa)

Your servants in Montreal,

## Morelia

I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is all-beautiful, having taken shelter at the lotus feet of Viṣṇu, and who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa. His mission is preaching the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. By the order of his spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī, he is delivering the entire world, especially the Western countries of *mlecchas, yavanas* and what-nots, from the pathos of impersonalism and voidism.

Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura, who appeared in the middle of the seventeenth century, has given us a beautiful prayer glorifying the spiritual master. He says that material existence, consisting of repeated birth and death, is like a great forest fire. In such a condition, only a cloud can alleviate the calamity. The cloud is a creation of the sun, which evaporates water from the ocean and land. Similarly, the spiritual master is a creation of Kṛṣṇa, and as such, he has obtained an ocean of auspicious qualities and is delivering the scorched people from the forest of material enjoyment out of his immense mercifulness.

The spiritual master from time to time experiences quivering in his body, torrents of tears, and hairs standing on end. This is due to the relishing of pure devotional service within his heart, the seat of the mind. He becomes exceedingly gladdened by the advancement of the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya, which consists of chanting and dancing and the accompaniment of various musical instruments such as *karatālas* and *mṛdaṅgas.* However, the *bṛhat mṛdaṅga,* or greater drum, is the publication and mass distribution of literature describing the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

The spiritual master engages his disciples in the traditional process of Deity worship, which is one of the nine kinds of pure devotional service. In its manifold expression, *arcanam* entails service rendered to Kṛṣṇa daily in His Deity form, along with various offerings of clothing and ornaments. Of equal stature is the cleansing of the temple and the maintenance of paraphernalia.

Apart from the altar, there is another temple, known as the *bhoga-mandira,* where food is stored and prepared for the pleasure of Kṛṣṇa. Four kinds of food, which are licked, chewed, drunk, and sucked, are offered to Kṛṣṇa at scheduled times throughout the day. The spiritual master has no desire to eat such palatable dishes simply for the satisfaction of his tongue and belly, but honors only a token of *prasādam* and becomes fully enlivened. The spiritual master is pleased to see the multitude of devotees satisfied with the remnants of food offered to Kṛṣṇa.

Hearing and chanting waters the creeper of devotion, and the spiritual master aspires to relish the unlimited conjugal pastimes of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, along with Their qualities, names, and forms.

In the beginning it was stated that the spiritual master is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa. This is because he is very expert in assisting the *gopīs* in their endeavor to make different tasteful arrangements for the perfection of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa's conjugal affairs.

The spiritual master is to be seen as God directly present. This is acknowledged in all revealed scriptures. Kṛṣṇa Himself told Uddhava just before His departure from the world that the spiritual master is He Himself. Kṛṣṇa warns us not to disrespect the spiritual master at any time. Neither should one be envious of the spiritual master or see him as being in a position like that of an ordinary, conditioned soul. Thus, the spiritual master is the representation of all the demigods. Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura confirms that all the revealed scriptures accept the spiritual master to be identical with the Personality of Godhead because he is a very dear and confidential servant of the Lord, as previously described in connection with his position as assistant to the *gopis.*

Gaudiya Vaiṣṇavas worship Śrīla Gurudeva in light of his being the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead. In all the ancient literature of devotional service, and in the more recent songs of Śrīla Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura, Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, and other unalloyed devotees, the spiritual master is always considered to be either one of the confidential associates of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī or a manifested representation of Śrīla Nityānanda Prabhu.

Thus, by the grace of Śrīla Prabhupāda one receives the mercy of Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. However can one make advancement in spiritual life by any other means? Let us meditate on our spiritual master three times a day and become enthused in executing the ISKCON movement.

(Written by Nirantara dāsa)

*The devotees of Morelia, Mexico, Mandira.*

## Nagpur

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

PLEASE accept our prostrated obeisances at your divine lotus feet. It is our great pleasure each year to write a Vyāsa-pūjā offering glorifying your divine self and your unlimited qualities and accomplishments. We pray to Kṛṣṇa to give us more and more realization of your incomparable attributes.

We would like to thank you eternally, again and again, for giving us the most wonderful opportunity to serve you through the preaching assignment of delivering the fallen conditioned souls. As we hear the preaching reports coming in from around the world, we become very excited about how this entire planet is actually becoming transformed into a Kṛṣṇa conscious world right before our eyes. But sometimes we feel w s lack the potency to save them or even ourselves from the grinding wheel of material existence.

So please empower us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to strictly follow your guidelines and mercifully bestow Kṛṣṇa consciousness on everyone we meet.

We beg you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to please send your special blessings to us, to your growing spiritual family of humble servants here in Nagpur. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your humble servants of Nagpur temple,

## New Delhi

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
Please accept our humble obeisances.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

This year ISKCON Delhi ushers in your Prabhupāda Nāgarī: "Glory of India Cultural Center."

Delhi is a place of your transcendental *līlās* and activities: "Those were hard days." You were struggling to print B.T.G. ... to save the world. Kumar Jain, the printer in Delhi, spoke about you. Although he thought your endeavors to be hopeless, he admired your conviction. At that time you were concealed by the obscurity of the Kali-yuga.

Mr. Jain would now be in shock to find such a great movement started and sustained by such a humbled and seemingly impoverished personality. But didn't Lord Kṛṣṇa work the same way with Arjuna and the Pāṇḍavas? You quoted: *yasyāham anugṛhṇāmi, hariṣye tad dhanaṁ śanaiḥ.* Kṛṣṇa wanted to dramatize your efforts by putting you in so much difficulty so that we would not forget how you struggled for us, and so that the world would appreciate, "Yes, he has done something great."

So now, Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is our duty to depict your true glory. We have purchased three acres situated on a hillock exactly where you had hoped for—the wealthy south Delhi area. A museum and library will be created to exhibit and focus on your great works, along with the works of Madhvācārya, Rāmānujācārya, and so on. A beautiful computerized museum, depicting the *Mahābhārata* and *Rāmāyaṇa,* and a superbly grand temple of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Pārthasārathi will also be constructed. At the moment, people in India are mad after such misguided fools as Rāma Krishna and Vivekananda, the modern-day fathers of impersonalism, but our duty and commitment is to change all that and present to them the father of modern *acintya-bhedābheda-tattva—*His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

You once said that India is covered by a thin veneer of *māyā.* We are seeing that many members from the higher and middle strata of society come forward and take initiation. *Yad yad ācārati śreṣṭhaḥ:* Whatever great men do, the common men will follow. Therefore eventually the whole of India can become inundated by Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana* movement. We see how many of the leaders in Delhi are Vedāntists—mostly Vaiṣṇavas—and when these young American boys and girls preach to them from the authorized Sanskrit texts, they take it very seriously, become obliged to surrender to Lord Kṛṣṇa's lotus movement.

I quote a letter to Tamāla Krishna Goswami, August 20, 1971: "Delhi is the most important city in India because the capital is established there. Mostly the population is educated. Many foreigners are there also and embassies of different countries. So from the cultural point of view, Delhi is the most important city ..."

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray to invoke your mercy and inspiration that we may do something for Lord Kṛṣṇa and his pure devotee—your good self.

In conclusion, Prabhupāda, we reflect on these wonderful qualities which are found in full within you:

*yasmān nodvijate loko*  
*lokān nodvijate ca yaḥ*  
*harṣāmarṣa-bhayodvegair*  
*mukto yaḥ sa ca me priyaḥ*

**"He for whom no one is put into difficulty and who is not disturbed by anyone, who is equipoised in happiness and distress, fear and anxiety, is very dear to Me."**

*anapekṣaḥ śucir daksa*  
*udāsīno gata-vyathaḥ*  
*sarvārambha-parityāgī*  
*yo mad-bhaktaḥ sa me priyaḥ*

**"My devotee who is not dependent on the ordinary course of activities, who is pure, expert, without cares, free from all pains, and not striving for some result, is very dear to Me."**

And we hope that we will one day become like you.

*Your servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Pārthasārathi Mandira,*

## New Gauḍa-deśa, Australia

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances. In separation from you and desiring to be your eternal servant, I have composed this short prayer.

Prabhupāda in my heart  
Giving knowledge, no more dark  
With your light shone around  
No avidyā; homeward bound.

Stumble and fall  
Time to time  
Getting strength  
Your message sublime.

So much work  
No time to fret  
How to help them  
Māyā forget.

Book in hand  
A warrior's stance  
Armed to defend us  
From illusion's glance.

You stand alone  
For time to tell  
Came from Kṛṣṇa  
Took us from hell.

Merciful master  
Smile on me  
I need your help  
Still I am not free.

(Written by Krodheśvara dāsa)

Your eternal servants,

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Krodheśvara dāsa

## New Govardhana, Ghana

Your image, your form, do we so often mourn, is here no longer with us.  
But vāṇī is greater, and your words are pure nectar for those who are returning to dust.

I could not go on Pādayātrā. My reasons are honest, but I know you are pleased in your heart  
To see pious Hindus offer respect to foreign sādhus who walk and chant alongside the sacred cart,  
Raising up the holy name, drawing the sentiments forth from the residents of Bhārata, who have the best chance for devotional life.  
I wish there were thousands of Pādayātrā teams to move along the roads of all India chanting Hari-nāma and removing all strife,  
As for the West, they like an opulent show of foreign culture and exotic foods for their tongues to savor.  
We have seen Kīrtanānanda, Tamāla Kṛṣṇa, and Ambarīṣa demonstrate this trick of Vaiṣṇava intelligence with great fervor.  
Those who cannot respond to the sound of Hari-nāma as in the East react better to marble and onyx halls with furled silk  
drapes hanging by Lord Kṛṣṇa's divine form.  
And a classy restaurant with sacred bhaktins offering every palatable dish, for which they pay nicely, as is the norm.  
If they ever knew they were becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious just by entering our sacred halls and downing Kṛṣṇa-prasādam, surely  
they would open up a Supreme Court injunction.  
But they are innocent and unintelligent, so what they do not know is our mercy on the conditioned soul, who does not know how to function.  
Prabhupāda, help us to fill the structure you made with enthusiasm and love, by which many more will become addicted to śrī nāma-kīrtana.  
You left us so empty, with no father, but the memory lives on as I write this song and hope one day to meet you in the Lord's own dhāma.  
I close now and ask that you help me another year to keep strict to the principles you set forth  
And never fall prey to māyā’s attacks, which want always to stain the Vaiṣṇava's cloth.  
Your lotus feet are saving grace, the dust which I always happily smear over my face.  
Your lonely son cries out for mercy and solace.

Your servants of ISKCON New Govardhana (Volta Region, Ghana).

## New Kurukṣetra, Caddo

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.*

IN NEW YORK CITY, a man once approached you while you were sitting on a park bench. You spoke to him as follows: "I am not a poor man, I am rich. There are temples and there are books. They are existing, they are there . . ." And, as you had envisioned, there are now hundreds of temples and millions of books all around the world.

Why were these temples and books important to you? They were important because you were to teach through the instruments of your writings and activities within your temples that, just as you were never alone, always secure in the wealth of Kṛṣṇa's friendship, we also may take assurance that we may also always be sheltered by Kṛṣṇa, be it in His temple, where we may offer personal service to Kṛṣṇa and worship and praise, or be it within one's very own heart, where Kṛṣṇa has personally resided throughout so many births and in so many bodies. This knowledge is such a great wealth, so simple, so beautiful, and available to everyone, in any situation.

It is the shelter of this pure knowledge that will enable us to continue in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, never forgetting Kṛṣṇa. We must beg humbly to always remain within the realm of your ever-present mercy. Please allow us to assist your mission, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your insignificant granddisciples at New Kurukṣetra,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda and present ISKCON** ***ācāryas***

Gopāla dāsa / Śukānanda dāsa / Nandapriyā-devī dāsī / Sītā-devī dāsī / Rāma / Tulasī / Mādhava / Rohiṇī

## New Orleans

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

PLEASE accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your Divine Grace has given us the mercy of Kṛṣṇa and made it easily accessible to everyone. This feat is unparalleled in the history of the world. So many great saints and sages have been empowered to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout certain areas of India. However, you broke open the flood-gates of mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Now everyone is drenched in the great nectar of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, no one can match your incredible preaching, for your are the one who has spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness everywhere. The feat has already been accomplished. Now, as you have built the framework, it is our service to fill it in. It is only by your mercy that we can do anything to spread this transcendental movement.

Here in New Orleans we are trying to spread your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement by preaching programs, Deity worship, *prasādam* distribution, and especially book distribution. We have an ideal preaching situation, because the New Talavan farm is only one hour away. So the bigger part of our community is developing there. In the past two years we have established a *gurukula* along with our other farm projects, which has made the community grow quite rapidly. When you visited here you gave many directions on how to develop things, and we are working in that direction. Many of our disciples stay here, as well as many granddisciples and children. Many of us have been working together for the past ten years or more to establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness in this area. There are so many devotees working together in a very close, happy atmosphere. It is very nice for householders because of the *gurukula* and different businesses they can take part in. And there is so much opportunity for preaching and book distribution.

The facility you have given us is so wonderful. Please give us the strength to serve you properly by expanding it unlimitedly.

*Your servants at Saṅkīrtana-pura (New Orleans),*

## New Ramaṇa-retī, Gainesville

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

PLEASE accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. To properly glorify such an exalted devotee as you is a difficult task. You have so mercifully brought the teachings of Lord Caitanya to the whole world. Thus you have fulfilled the prediction of Lord Caitanya: "My holy name will be heard in every town and village." This is proof that you are an empowered servant of Lord Caitanya. As stated in *Caitanya-caritāmṛta, kṛṣṇe-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana:* "Unless one is specifically empowered by Kṛṣṇa, he cannot spread the teachings of the Lord." We are trying to follow in your footsteps by executing your instructions and preaching to the conditioned souls. By your mercy we can give up our material desires and become successful in carrying out your mission to give all fallen souls the opportunity of devotional service. This is the only way in which we can attempt to repay you for saving us from the darkest regions of hellish life. Please give us the strength to continue in devotional service, despite all obstacles.

Your unworthy servants at New Ramaṇa-reti,
